## Required GTM Tables

The following tables must be confirmed in the live database:

```text
bf_investment_scraper
bf_investment_trade_alerts
bf_investment_tickers
bf_investment_alert_history
Required GTM Behaviors
1. Scraped records start as In Review.
2. Scraped records are marked Processed after successful processing.
3. Tickers are extracted from subject/body.
4. Missing tickers are inserted into bf_investment_tickers.
5. New alerts are inserted into bf_investment_trade_alerts.
6. Existing alerts increment occurrences instead of creating duplicate rows.
7. Alert history snapshots are inserted.
8. Notifications are not sent more than once for the same alert.
9. Market-data API calls are not made excessively.
10. Dashboard filters and pagination work.
Initial GTM Priority
P1: Alert pipeline stability
P2: Alert dashboard visibility
P3: Discord/email distribution
P4: TBI Marketing promotional CTA
P5: News enrichment
P6: Daily digest
